Alternatively, optimize sum of top c powers. Better to implement actual scalar objective with FFT and Adam on logit. Laying out code robust:

local objective:

def conv_full_fft(a, L):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=L)[:2*n-1]
    return c

def score_fft(a):
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-9: return inf
    c=conv_full_fft(a,L)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return (2*n*np.max(c))/(s*s)

For smooth surrogate:
def smooth_grad(a, beta):
   c=conv...
   shift=c.max()
   e=np.exp(beta*(c-shift))
   w=e/e.sum()
   # w length 2n-1, pad to L
   wL=np.zeros(L); wL[:2n-1]=w
   W=np.fft.rfft(wL)
   G=np.fft.irfft(W*np.fft.rfft(a,L)).real[:n]
   # grad_a = 2*G? If using S=Σ c_k log? For S=Σ exp(β c), derivative =βΣ exp(βc) = β e? If weight p_k=β e_k/Z times? Let's define minimize logsum: weights w_k=e_k/Z, grad_a=2*conv(β w,a) =2β conv(w,a). Multiplicative update scale η absorbs β.
   grad=2*G (maybe beta omitted)
   # Normalize objective scale invariant, but sum fixed; we can subtract mean normalized gradient.
   log_grad = grad / a (where a>0). smoothing: a may zero; use eps.
   return log_grad

Let's consider implementing the SOTA LP with scipy on n~512-1024, after using our smooth optimizer to get a good nonuniform start with F maybe 1.9. LP can further reduce. How to use LP? Need scipy available. Let's design exact LP solve.

LP:
primal maximize sum_g = 1^T g
A g <= M * 1
g >=0
where A size (2n-1,n), A_{k,i}=f_i if 0≤k-i<n.
M = max conv(f,f) (with sum=1) = score/(2n). f is current best. Use scipy linprog(c=-ones, A_ub=A, b_ub=M*ones, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'dual_rel_gap':1e-9}). Need A as sparse for efficiency. HiGHS supports sparse. But constructing A with f values repeated in Toeplitz. For n=512, dense A float64 1023*512=0.5M -> 4MB, fine. For n=1024, 2M ->16MB; okay. For n=2048, 8M ->64MB; maybe okay but LP slower. Use n=512 for LP. If final sequence length 512 achieves <1.5, great. If not, we can downsample/up-sample? Need quality.

LP may find g not finite if M too small? f feasible. We can add small tolerance: b=M*ones + 1e-12.

    x = np.log(a + TINY)

`normalize_pos(arr)`:
    arr = np.asarray(arr, float).ravel()
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = arr.sum()
    if s <= 0: arr = np.ones_like(arr); s=arr.size
    arr /= s
    return arr

`fft_convolution(a, L)`:
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=L)[:2*a.size-1]
    # small negative to 0
    c = np.real(c)
    np.minimum(c, 0.0, out=c) ? Actually np.clip(c,0,None,out=c)
    return c

`smooth_step(a, L, gamma, eta, mu, rng, max_v)`:
    n=a.size
    A = np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)[:2*n-1]
    c = max(c,0)
    m = c.max()
    if m<=0: return a, c, 2n*m? 
    r = c/m
    # weights: w = exp(gamma*(r-1)) 
    # To avoid underflow, we can use `w = np.power(r, p)` if gamma not exp. Using exp of negative up to -gamma; if gamma<=100 okay underflow to 0 fine.
    w = np.exp(gamma * (r - 1.0))
    s = w.sum()
    if not finite or s==0: w = np.ones_like(r); s = len(r)
    w /= s
    # mix with uniform and maybe previous?
    if mu >0:
       w = (1-mu)*w + mu/(len(w))
    # optional smooth weights with FFT? not needed
    wL = zeros(L); wL[:len(w)] = w
    W = rfft(wL)
    G = irfft(W*A,n=L).real[:n]
    # G nonnegative? Could be tiny negative due FFT; clip 0
    np.clip(G,0,None,out=G)
    # v = G / mean_a
    mean_a = a.mean()
    v = G / (mean_a + EPS)
    v -= v.mean()
    # Add anti-symmetric mode if v flat
    var = v.std()
    if var < 1e-8:
       # use deterministic anti-symmetric low freq
       x = np.arange(n)
       s = np.sin(2*np.pi*x/n + np.pi/4) maybe anti? sine phase 0 anti. Use anti-sym s_i = sin(2πx/n) (if n even)
       s = s - s.mean()
       v = 0.01 * (s - s.mean())
    v = np.clip(v, -max_v, max_v)
    x = np.log(a + TINY)
    x = x - eta * v
    x = x - x.max()
    b = np.exp(x)
    bsum = b.sum()
    if bsum<=0: b = a.copy()
    else: b /= bsum
    # optionally small random jitter
    if rng is not None and random_jitter:
       jitter = rng.normal(0, jitter_amp, n)
       b *= np.exp(jitter)
       b /= b.sum()
    return b, c, 2*n*c.max()
```

   b = M * np.ones(2n-1) + 1e-12

while time:
  cand=best.copy()
  width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//2000), max(4,n//20)+1))
  start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
  cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,size=width))
  if rng.random()<0.2:

  score=score_fft(cand)
  if score < best_score: best=cand; best_score=score
  else: maybe try smaller noise
```

```
def full_grid_optimize(a, budget_s, seed=0, gamma_start=6, gamma_end=80, max_iters_high=20000, label=''):
    n=a.size; L=next_pow2_int(2*n-1)
    cur=normalize_nonnegative(a)
    x = np.log(cur + TINY)
    x -= x.max(); x=np.clip(x,-30,30)
    cur_score = score_from_x(x,L,n)
    best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic()+budget_s
    eta=0.7
    mu=0.08
    max_v=4.0
    noise_scale=0.02
    no_improve=0
    gamma=gamma_start
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       if no_improve>800:
          # restart from best with a random small lowfreq perturbation or increase gamma
          best_x = np.log(best+TINY); best_x-=best_x.max(); best_x=np.clip(best_x,-30,30)
          pert = rng.normal(0,0.05,n); # maybe lowpass?
          x = best_x + 0.5*pert
          cur = softmax_from_x(x); cur_score = score_from_x(...)
          no_improve=0
          gamma = min(gamma_end, gamma*1.3)
       v, cur_score, m = compute_v_and_score(x, L, gamma, mu, noise_scale, rng)
       if not finite v: no_improve += 1000; continue
       eta_try = eta
       accepted=False
       for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
           x_trial = x - eta_try*factor*v
           x_trial = np.clip(x_trial, -30, 30) # but subtract max after
           b = softmax_from_x(x_trial)
           sc = score_from_x_from_a? we can compute score from b (need FFT again)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
               x=x_trial; cur=b; cur_score=sc; accepted=True; break
       if accepted:
           if cur_score < best_score:
               best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score; print...
           eta=min(2.0, eta*1.02)
           no_improve=0
       else:
           # try a random direction
           d = rng.normal(0,0.05,n)
           # maybe lowpass
           x_trial = x + d
           b=softmax...
           sc=score...
           if sc < cur_score:
              ...
           else:
              no_improve += 1
              eta *= 0.9
              if eta < 0.05: break? 
```

Now, anti-symmetric fallback: Need a vector `s` of length n with anti-sym if n odd/even. For even n, `s[i]=sin(2π i/n)` has s_{n-1-i} =? sin(2π(n-1-i)/n)=sin(2π - 2πi/n)= -sin? yes. For n odd, center? fine. We'll use `s = np.sin(2*np.pi*np.arange(n)/n)`; subtract mean; scale to std=1. Then `v = 0.1*(s - s.mean())`.
If `v` flat, gradient descent can't move. But if current is uniform, adding this direction might reduce or increase? From uniform, updating x = x - eta*v (with v=s) yields a=softmax(-eta*s). For eta positive, masses large where s negative. This is f=1 - eta sin; we know might help center but could increase elsewhere. The score may or may not decrease. If it doesn't, our line search rejects and random perturbations. But to escape uniform, we can allow a "sideways" move: if score not worse by more than tolerance, accept even if slightly worse? `compute_direction(a,L,gamma,mu,noise,rng)`:
   n = a.size
   A = np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
   c = np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)[:2*n-1].real
   np.clip(c,0,None,out=c)
   s = c.sum() # should 1 if normalized, but for score use s? Since a normalized; okay.
   m = c.max()
   if m <=0: return np.zeros(n), 2*n*m
   r = c/m
   if noise > 0 and rng is not None:
      # add small noise to non-max coefficients? 
      rn = r + noise * (rng.standard_normal(len(r)) - r.mean())
      rn = np.maximum(rn, 0.0)
      mn = rn.max()
      if mn > 0: r = rn/mn
   expv = np.exp(gamma*(r-1.0))
   ws = expv.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <=0:
       expv = np.ones_like(r); ws = len(r)
   w = expv/ws
   if mu >0:
       w = (1-mu)*w + mu/len(w)
   W = np.fft.rfft(w, n=L)
   G = np.fft.irfft(W*A,n=L).real[:n]
   np.clip(G,0,None,out=G)
   v = G/(a.mean()+EPS)
   v -= v.mean()
   if np.std(v) < 1e-10:
      s = np.sin(2*np.pi*np.arange(n)/max(1,n).real)
      s = s - s.mean(); sd = std; if sd>0: v = 0.05*s/sd
   v = np.clip(v, -6.0, 6.0)
   return v, 2.0*n*m
```
Score from normalized a =2n m (sum 1). If a not normalized, score_sequence_fft divides by sum². But normalizes in internal.

   b = np.exp(x2); bs=sum; if no return None,inf
   return b/bs, score_sequence_fft(b)

Now, upsample:
```
def upsample_sequence(a, new_n):
   a=normalize_nonnegative(a)
   old_n=a.size
   if new_n==old_n: return a
   if new_n < old_n:
      old_idx = np.linspace(0, old_n, new_n, endpoint=False)
      # interpolate log
      loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
      b = np.interp(old_idx, np.arange(old_n), loga)
   else:
      old_idx = np.linspace(0, old_n, new_n, endpoint=False)
      loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
      b = np.interp(old_idx, np.arange(old_n), loga)
   return normalize_nonnegative(np.exp(b-b.max()))
```
Use periodic? Interpolation endpoint false; last point before 1 not connected to first. Convolution boundaries not periodic; okay. Could reflect to preserve endpoints: use x_old = np.linspace(0,1,old_n), x_new. `np.interp` with default. Good.

Build A:
```
def build_conv_matrix(a):
   n=a.size
   A = np.empty((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
   for k in range(2*n-1):
       i0 = max(0,k-n+1); i1 = min(k,n-1)+1
       A[k,i0:i1]=a[i0:i1]
   return A
```
Check dimensions: For k=0, i0=0,i1=1 -> A[0,0]=a0. Convolution (f*g)_0 = f0 g0. Good. k=1: i0=0,i1=2 -> A[1,0]=f0, A[1,1]=f1 => f0g1+f1g0. Correct.
Use dense. For n>1200 skip.

Let's detail `_fourier_fun` robust:
```
def _fourier_fun(par):
    global _FH, _L, _N, _BETA
    n=_N; H=_FH; L=_L; beta=_BETA
    x = H @ par
    z = x - x.max(); z=np.clip(z,-30,30)
    e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(par)
    a=e/s
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)[:2*n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
    cmax=c.max()
    if cmax<=0: return 0, zeros
    b = beta*(c - cmax)
    # clip b < -700 to avoid underflow? exp(-1000)=0 fine
    eb=np.exp(b)
    zsum=eb.sum()
    if zsum <=0: return np.inf, zeros
    val=np.log(zsum)
    p=eb/zsum
    wL? W=np.fft.rfft(p,n=L)
    G=np.fft.irfft(W*A,n=L).real[:n]
    G=np.maximum(G,0)
    grad_a=2.0*G
    grad_x=a*(grad_a - np.dot(a,grad_a))
    grad=H.T @ grad_x
    # sanitize
    grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0)
    return val, grad
```
The objective val = logsumexp(beta(c-cmax)). The gradient wrt a is 2 beta p * conv? Wait derivative of logsum wrt c = β p. Then derivative wrt a = 2 conv(β p, a). I set p=eb/Z and forgot β in grad_a. Need `grad_a = 2.0 * beta * G` if G=conv(p,a). Include beta.
If beta large (e.g. 50), grad_a scaled. L-BFGS handles.
`val` large? logsum of exp ≤? At cmax, eb=1 at max maybe; logsum≥0. Good.

Now, if H@par has large values, softmax okay. L-BFGS may drift coefficients large; val maybe. We can set `maxls` etc. Use options: `maxiter=300, ftol=1e-12, gtol=1e-8, maxls=40`. If fail, return last x? We can just use `optimize_fourier` to track best from each callback? SciPy no callback for state. It returns best.

def make_base_start(n, seed):
   x = np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
   starts=[]
   starts.append(np.ones(n))
   # skew reciprocal
   for alpha in [0.5,1,2]: starts.append(1/(1+alpha*x))
   # skew left-right
   starts.append(0.7/(1+ x) + 0.3/(1+(1-x)))
   # sine variants
   starts.append(1 + 0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*(x+seed*0.05)))
   starts.append(1 + 0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*x + 0.7))

    a=normalize_nonnegative(start)

    deadline=...

       if time > deadline: break
       M = best_score/(2*n)

       except: break

       g=res.x

       g=np.clip(g,0,None); s=g.sum(); if s<=0: break
       g/=s
       # line search

           mix=(1-t)*best + t*g
           sc=score_sequence_fft(mix)
           if sc < cur_score - 1e-10:

       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-9:
           best=cur_best; best_score=cur_score
           print
       else:
           # Try smaller M? or break
           break
    return best, best_score

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _sp_minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT = False
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _sp_linprog
    HAVE_SCIPY_LP = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY_LP = False

_TINY=1e-300
_EPS=1e-12
_BASE_EPS=1e-300

_FH=None; _FL=0; _FN=0; _FBETA=10.0

def next_pow2_int(x):
    x=int(max(1,x))
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()

def as_1d_float(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return a

def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    a=as_1d_float(a)
    if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 0.0:
       return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
    return a/s
```
Careful: if a empty, we return [1] but caller expects length? For initial. Could return None? Use if empty.

`softmax_log(x)`:
```
def softmax_from_x(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float).ravel()
    z=x - x.max()
    z=np.clip(z,-30,30)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if not finite or s<=0: return normalize_nonnegative(x)
    return e/s
```
If x all -inf? np.max -inf; handle finite.

`convolution_fft(a,L)`:
```
def convolution_fft(a,L=None):
    a=as_1d_float(a)
    n=a.size
    if L is None: L=next_pow2_int(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)[:2*n-1].real
    np.clip(c,0.0,None,out=c)
    return c
```
`A*A` complex; product of one-sided as discussed. Good.

`score_sequence_fft(a)`:
```
def score_sequence_fft(a):
    a=as_1d_float(a)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    s=a.sum()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if s < 1e-12: return np.inf
    c=convolution_fft(a)
    m=c.max()
    if not finite: return inf
    return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
```
For normalized sum1, same.

`direct_score(a)` (for final check small):
```
def direct_score(a):
    a=as_1d_float(a); a=np.maximum(a,0); n=a.size; s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12 inf
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    return float(2*n*np.max(c)/(s*s))
```

`upsample_sequence(a,new_n)`:
```
def upsample_sequence(a,new_n):
    a=normalize_nonnegative(a)
    old_n=a.size
    if new_n == old_n: return a
    new_n=max(2,int(new_n))
    loga=np.log(a + 1e-300)
    old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
    new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
    val=np.interp(new_x, old_x, loga)
    val -= val.max(); val=np.clip(val,-30,30)
    b=np.exp(val); b/=b.sum()
    return b
```
If old has zeros, log -inf -> clip? Better `loga=np.log(a + 1e-300)`, large negative; interp okay. `val` maybe huge negative; clip -30. Good.

`make_start(n,seed)`:
We need top-level no closures. It can call score_sequence_fft many times.
```
def make_start_sequence(n, seed=42):
    n=max(2,int(n))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    best=None; bestsc=np.inf
    candidates=[]
    candidates.append(np.ones(n))
    candidates.append(1.0/(1.0+x))
    candidates.append(1.0/(1.0+0.5*x))
    candidates.append(1.0/(1.0+2.0*x))
    candidates.append(1.0/(1.0+x) + 0.5*(1.0/(1.0+(1.0-x))))
    candidates.append(1.0 + 0.85*np.sin(2*np.pi*(x + rng.random())))
    candidates.append(1.0 + 0.85*np.sin(2*np.pi*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)))
    # low-freq random
    M=4
    i=np.arange(n)
    for m in range(1,M+1):
       c=rng.normal(0,0.8); s=rng.normal(0,0.8)
       candidates.append(np.exp(c*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*i/n)+ s*np.sin(...)))
    for cand in candidates:
       b=normalize_nonnegative(cand)
       sc=score_sequence_fft(b)
       if sc<bestsc: best=b; bestsc=sc
    return best
```
This computes scores for many n; maybe okay for n up to 4096 (12 starts * score FFT length 8192 -> maybe <1s). Good.
Need ensure nonnegative: `1+0.85 sin` nonnegative. For random exp, positive.

`lowfreq Random` can be just inside make_start. No nested.

`sequence_to_fourier_coeff(a,H)`:
```
def project_sequence_to_fourier(a,H):
    a=normalize_nonnegative(a)
    x=np.log(a+1e-300)
    coef, residuals, rank, sv = np.linalg.lstsq(H,x,rcond=None)
    return coef
```
If H shape (n,p). For n large, lstsq O(n p) okay.

`make_fourier_matrix(n,M)`:
```
def make_fourier_matrix(n,M):
    i=np.arange(n)
    cols=[]
    for m in range(1,M+1):
       cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*m*i/n))
       cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*m*i/n))
    H=np.column_stack(cols)
    return H
```
If M=0, H shape (n,0) but we won't.

`_fourier_fun` as above.
Need set `_FH` etc.

`optimize_fourier(n,M,beta,seed,start_seq,maxiter)`:
```
def optimize_fourier(n, M, beta, seed, start_seq=None, maxiter=200):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY_OPT:
       if start_seq is not None: return normalize_nonnegative(start_seq), score_sequence_fft(...)
    H=make_fourier_matrix(n,max(1,int(M)))
    L=next_pow2_int(2*n-1)
    _FH=H; _FL=L; _FN=n; _FBETA=float(beta)
    if start_seq is not None:
       x0=project_sequence_to_fourier(start_seq,H)
    else:
       rng=default_rng(seed); x0=rng.normal(0,0.6,H.shape[1])
    x0=np.clip(x0,-3,3)
    bounds=[(-3.0,3.0)]*H.shape[1]
    try:
       res=_sp_minimize(_fourier_fun, x0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-7, 'maxls':30})
       xpar = res.x if res.x is not None else x0
    except Exception:
       xpar=x0
    a=softmax_from_x(H @ xpar)
    sc=score_sequence_fft(a)
    if not finite:
       if start_seq is not None: return normalize(start), score
       return make_start(n, seed), score
    return a, sc
```
Could also evaluate score of x0 if start better? We can compare.

`full_grid_optimize` is core. Let's design with clear parameters:
`full_grid_optimize(start, budget_s, seed, gamma_start=6.0, gamma_end=80.0, mu_start=0.15, eta_start=0.8, max_v=4.0):`
Returns (a,best_score).
Implementation details:
- Convert start to normalized, n,L.
- `cur` and `x`.
- best.
- deadline.
- If time budget <=0 return.
- We will do stages by gamma: `stages = [(gamma_start, 0.25),(gamma_start**0.5? no)]`. Maybe simpler:
   total = budget_s
   gamma = gamma_start
   stage_budgets = [0.35,0.35,0.30]
   for fraction in stage_budgets:
      stage_end = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+total*fraction_remaining?) But if previous stages not full, distribute remaining. Better:
```
remaining = deadline - now
fractions=[0.4,0.35,0.25]
for idx,f in enumerate(fractions):
   stage_end = min(deadline, now + remaining*f)
   gamma = gamma_start*(gamma_end/gamma_start)**(idx/(len-1))
   run loop until stage_end
```
- Inside loop:
```
no_impr=0
eta=eta_start
mu = mu_start*(1-idx/3)
while now<stage_end:
   v, cur_score = compute_direction(cur,L,gamma,mu,0.03,rng)
   if not finite: no_impr += 100; continue
   accepted=False
   for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
       b, sc = candidate_from_x(x - eta*fac*v, L,n)
       if finite and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
           cur=b; x=log; cur_score=sc; accepted=True; break
       elif finite and sc < cur_score + 1e-8 and no_impr > 200:
           # accept neutral to explore? maybe skip
           pass
   if accepted:
       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-8:
           best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score; print
       no_impr=0
       eta=min(2.0, eta*1.03)
   else:
       no_impr += 1
       if no_impr % 50 ==0:
          eta *=0.9
       if no_impr > 800:
          # restart from best with low-freq perturbation
          if best is not None:
             xb = np.log(best+eps); xb-=xb.max(); xb=np.clip(xb,-30,30)
             pert = rng.normal(0,0.15,n)
             # lowpass pert? maybe just
             x = xb + pert
             cur = softmax_from_x(x); cur_score = score_sequence_fft(cur)
          no_impr=0
          eta=eta_start
```
Need if `cur_score` from compute_direction uses a normalized a with sum? We normalize. Good.

def local_perturb_sequence(start,budget_s,seed):
   a=normalize_nonnegative(start); n=L...
   best=a,bests=score
   deadline...
   rng...
   while time<deadline:

      s=int(rng.integers(0,n-width))
      cand=a.copy()

def lp_refine_sequence(start, budget_s, seed=0, max_iters=200):
   if not HAVE_SCIPY_LP: return start,score
   n=...; if n<24 or n>1100: return
   a=normalize; L=...; score=...
   deadline...
   t_choices=[0.002,0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0]
   objs=-np.ones(n)
   bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
   best=a; bests=score
   no_impr=0
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time > deadline: break
      if no_impr > 20: break
      M = bests/(2*n)
      # If M tiny, add eps
      A = build as rows slice
      b = np.full(2*n-1, M*1.00000001 + 1e-12)
      try:
         res=_sp_linprog(objs, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
      except: break
      if res.status not in [0] or res.x is None: break
      g=as_1d_float(res.x); g=np.maximum(g,0); s=g.sum(); if s<eps: break
      g/=s
      bestmix=best; bestscore=bests
      for t in t_choices:
         mix=(1-t)*best + t*g
         sc=score_sequence_fft(mix)
         if sc < bestscore - 1e-10:
             bestscore=sc; bestmix=mix
      if bestscore < bests - 1e-8:
         best=bestmix; bests=bestscore; no_impr=0; print
      else:
         no_impr+=1
   return best,bests
```
Build A in loop each iter. Could avoid building if M changes? Must. Efficient build:

A = np.empty((2*n-1,n), dtype=float)

```

Need ensure `_fourier_fun` gradient uses `beta` and `G` exactly. Let's implement:
```
def _fourier_fun(par):
    global _FH, _FL, _FN, _FBETA
    n = _FN; H=_FH; L=_FL; beta=_FBETA
    x=H.dot(par)
    z=x - z.max(); z=np.clip(z,-30,30)
    e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite: return 1e100, np.zeros(H.shape[1])
    a=e/s
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)[:2*n-1].real
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    cmax=c.max()
    if cmax <= 0: return 0.0, zeros
    bc = beta * c
    # if max too large, subtract but then gradient wrong; cap
    if bc.max() > 50.0:
       bc = bc - bc.max()  # approximate, rare
    eb=np.exp(bc)
    Z=eb.sum()
    if Z <=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
    val=float(np.log(Z))
    p=eb/Z
    W=np.fft.rfft(p,n=L)
    G=np.fft.irfft(W*A,n=L).real[:n]
    G=np.maximum(G,0.0)
    grad_a=2.0*beta*G
    axg = a*grad_a
    grad_x = axg - a*np.dot(a, axg?) Wait derivative wrt softmax: grad_x_i = a_i grad_a_i - a_i * sum(a_j grad_a_j). So Sdot = np.dot(a, grad_a). grad_x = a * (grad_a - Sdot). Equivalent.
    Sdot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_x = a * (grad_a - Sdot)
    grad = H.T.dot(grad_x)
    grad=np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad,0.0)
    return val, grad
```
Note `H.T.dot(grad_x)` dimensions. `axg` not needed.

For n=2, uniform cmax=0.5; nonuniform cmax up to1; β=700 ->700 safe. We'll use β<=100. bc<=100, exp 2.7e43, sum maybe 1e45, safe. No subtract needed. Good. def update_state(seq, score, state):

    if score is None or not finite: score=score_sequence_fft(seq)
    if score < state[1] - 1e-12:
       state[1]=score; state[0]=seq; print("GLOBAL BEST ...")
```

    if seq.sum() < 0.01: seq = seq* (0.02/max(seq.sum(),1e-12))
    return [float(x) for x in seq]

Let's detail `full_grid_optimize` code with time stages:
```
def full_grid_optimize(start, budget_s, seed=0, gamma_start=6.0, gamma_end=80.0, eta_start=0.8, mu_start=0.15, max_v=5.0):
    if budget_s <=0: return start, score
    start=normalize_nonnegative(start)
    n=start.size; L=next_pow2_int(2*n-1)
    cur=start.copy()
    x=np.log(cur+TINY); x-=x.max(); x=np.clip(x,-30,30)
    cur_score=score_sequence_fft(cur)
    best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score
    deadline = time.monotonic()+max(0,float(budget_s))
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    eta=eta_start
    no_impr=0
    stage_fracs=[0.45,0.35,0.20]
    stage_end_global=deadline
    for idx, frac in enumerate(stage_fracs):
        stage_end = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + (deadline - time.monotonic())*frac)
        gamma = gamma_start * (gamma_end/gamma_start)**(idx/(len(stage_fracs)-1))
        mu = max(0.0, mu_start * (1.0 - 0.5*idx/len(stage_fracs)))
        while time.monotonic() < stage_end:
            v, cur_score = compute_direction(cur, L, gamma, mu, 0.02, rng)
            if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
                no_impr += 200
                continue
            accepted=False
            for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
                x_trial = x - (eta*fac)*v
                b, sc = candidate_from_x(x_trial, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                    cur=b; x=np.log(cur+TINY); x-=x.max(); x=np.clip(x,-30,30); cur_score=sc; accepted=True; break
            if accepted:
                no_impr=0
                if cur_score < best_score - 1e-10:
                    best=cur.copy(); best_score=cur_score
                    if best_score < best_score_threshold? print
                eta = min(2.5, eta*1.03)
            else:
                no_impr += 1
                eta = max(0.05, eta*0.95)
                if no_impr > 600:
                    # restart best with random low freq
                    xb = np.log(best + TINY); xb -= xb.max(); xb=np.clip(xb,-30,30)
                    pert = rng.normal(0,0.15, n)
                    x = xb + pert
                    cur = softmax_from_x(x)
                    cur_score = score_sequence_fft(cur)
                    no_impr=0
                    eta=eta_start
                    # Still if cur_score worse than best, subsequent will try improve from there.
        # end while
    return best, best_score
```

    """..."""

    if hs is not None:
        hs_arr = as_1d_float(hs)
        hs_arr = np.maximum(hs_arr,0.0)

        sc = score_sequence_fft(state_seq)
        update_state(state_seq, sc, state)
        print
    # helper remain
    def? no nested; use remaining_until(deadline)
```

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)**2).

The program combines:
  - seeded smooth Fourier/low-frequency initializers,
  - an optional L-BFGS-B optimizer in a low-dimensional Fourier basis,
  - a multiplicative log-space local search driven by exponential weights
    on the large convolution coefficients,
  - an LP direction refinement inspired by the previous state of the art,
  - hierarchical resampling to larger sequence lengths,
  - a small final local perturbation pass.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _sp_minimize
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT = False

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog as _sp_linprog
    HAVE_SCIPY_LP = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY_LP = False

_TINY = 1e-300
_EPS = 1e-12

# Globals used by the low-level Fourier L-BFGS objective.
_FH = None
_FL = 0
_FN = 0
_FBETA = 10.0


def next_pow2_int(x):
    """Smallest power of two >= x."""
    x = int(max(1, x))
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def as_1d_float(a):
    """Convert input to a 1D float64 array, replacing non-finite values by 0."""
    if a is None:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return a


def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    """Return a nonnegative 1D float array of sum 1.0."""
    a = as_1d_float(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        out = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        return out / out.size
    return a / s


def softmax_from_x(x):
    """Stable softmax of a 1D array."""
    x = as_1d_float(x)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    z = x - x.max()
    z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return normalize_nonnegative(x)
    return e / s


def convolution_fft(a, L=None):
    """Full linear convolution via zero-padded real FFT, clipped to nonnegative."""
    a = as_1d_float(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if L is None:
        L = next_pow2_int(2 * n - 1)
    else:
        L = next_pow2_int(max(int(L), 2 * n - 1))
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)[:2 * n - 1].real
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.clip(c, 0.0, None, out=c)
    return c


def score_sequence_fft_L(a, L):
    """Score using an already-known FFT length."""
    a = as_1d_float(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)[:2 * n - 1].real
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.clip(c, 0.0, None, out=c)
    m = c.max()
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float((2.0 * n * m) / (s * s))


def score_sequence_fft(a):
    """Score with automatic FFT length selection."""
    a = as_1d_float(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    L = next_pow2_int(2 * a.size - 1)
    return score_sequence_fft_L(a, L)


def direct_score(a):
    """Direct O(n^2) score, used only for small/final checks."""
    a = as_1d_float(a)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    s = a.sum()
    if n == 0 or s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    m = np.max(c)
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float((2.0 * n * m) / (s * s))


def safe_exact_score(seq):
    """Call the externally provided evaluate_sequence if available."""
    try:
        lst = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel().tolist()]
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def upsample_sequence(a, new_n):
    """Log-interpolate a nonnegative sequence to a new length, then renormalize."""
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    old_n = a.size
    new_n = max(2, int(new_n))
    if old_n < 2:
        return np.full(new_n, 1.0 / new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a

    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    val = np.interp(new_x, old_x, loga)
    val = np.where(np.isfinite(val), val, -30.0)
    val -= val.max()
    val = np.clip(val, -30.0, 30.0)
    b = np.exp(val)
    b = b / b.sum()
    return b


def make_fourier_matrix(n, M):
    """Cos/sin Fourier basis for frequencies 1..M."""
    n = max(2, int(n))
    M = max(1, int(M))
    i = np.arange(n)
    cols = []
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * i / n))
        cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * i / n))
    return np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64)


def project_sequence_to_fourier(a, H):
    """Least-squares projection of log(a) onto a Fourier basis."""
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    x = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    try:
        coef, _res, _rank, _sv = np.linalg.lstsq(H, x, rcond=None)
        coef = np.where(np.isfinite(coef), coef, 0.0)
    except Exception:
        coef = np.zeros(H.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    return np.clip(coef, -4.0, 4.0)


def _fourier_fun(par):
    """Objective and exact gradient for a low-dimensional Fourier parametrization."""
    global _FH, _FL, _FN, _FBETA
    n = _FN
    H = _FH
    L = _FL
    beta = _FBETA

    if H is None or n <= 0 or L <= 0 or H.shape[1] == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(0)

    x = H.dot(par)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(H.shape[1])

    a = softmax_from_x(x)
    zero_grad = np.zeros(H.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)[:2 * n - 1].real
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.clip(c, 0.0, None, out=c)
    cmax = c.max()
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        return 0.0, zero_grad

    # logsumexp(beta*c), no data-dependent shift, exact gradient.
    bc = beta * c
    # beta is kept modest in callers; this guard is only a numerical safety net.
    if bc.max() > 600.0:
        bc = bc - bc.max()
    eb = np.exp(bc)
    Z = eb.sum()
    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        return 1e100, zero_grad

    val = float(np.log(Z))
    p = eb / Z

    W = np.fft.rfft(p, n=L)
    G = np.fft.irfft(W * A, n=L).real[:n]
    G = np.where(np.isfinite(G), G, 0.0)
    np.clip(G, 0.0, None, out=G)

    grad_a = 2.0 * beta * G
    sdot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    if not np.isfinite(sdot):
        return val, zero_grad

    grad_x = a * (grad_a - sdot)
    grad_x = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_x), grad_x, 0.0)

    grad = H.T.dot(grad_x)
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return val, grad


def optimize_fourier(n, M, beta, seed, start_seq=None, maxiter=80):
    """Run L-BFGS-B on a low-dimensional Fourier basis, if SciPy is available."""
    n = max(2, int(n))
    M = max(1, int(M))
    if start_seq is not None:
        best_a = normalize_nonnegative(start_seq)
        best_sc = score_sequence_fft(best_a)
    else:
        best_a = make_start_sequence(n, seed)
        best_sc = score_sequence_fft(best_a)

    if not HAVE_SCIPY_OPT or maxiter <= 0:
        return best_a, best_sc

    H = make_fourier_matrix(n, M)
    L = next_pow2_int(2 * n - 1)

    global _FH, _FL, _FN, _FBETA
    _FH = H
    _FL = L
    _FN = n
    _FBETA = float(beta)

    if start_seq is not None:
        x0 = project_sequence_to_fourier(best_a, H)
    else:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        x0 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, size=H.shape[1])
    x0 = np.where(np.isfinite(x0), x0, 0.0)
    x0 = np.clip(x0, -4.0, 4.0)

    bounds = [(-4.0, 4.0)] * H.shape[1]
    try:
        res = _sp_minimize(
            _fourier_fun,
            x0,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options={"maxiter": max(1, int(maxiter)), "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-7, "maxls": 30},
        )
        xpar = res.x if res.x is not None else x0
    except Exception:
        xpar = x0

    xpar = np.where(np.isfinite(xpar), xpar, 0.0)
    a = softmax_from_x(H.dot(xpar))
    sc = score_sequence_fft(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        return a, sc
    return best_a, best_sc


def make_start_sequence(n, seed=42):
    """A small bank of smooth nonnegative starts; return the best one by FFT score."""
    n = max(2, int(n))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    i = np.arange(n)

    candidates = [
        np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
        1.0 / (1.0 + x),
        1.0 / (1.0 + 0.45 * x),
        1.0 / (1.0 + 1.8 * x),
        1.0 / (1.0 + x) + 0.35 / (1.0 + (1.0 - x)),
        1.0 + 0.85 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (x + rng.random())),
        1.0 + 0.85 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)),
        1.0 + 0.65 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * (x + rng.random())) + 0.35 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x),
    ]

    # Low-frequency random logit starts.
    for _ in range(3):
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 5):
            c = rng.normal(0.0, 0.75)
            s = rng.normal(0.0, 0.75)
            z += c * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * i / n) + s * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * i / n)
        candidates.append(np.exp(np.clip(z, -3.0, 3.0)))

    best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    best_sc = float("inf")
    for cand in candidates:
        a = normalize_nonnegative(cand)
        sc = score_sequence_fft(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a
    return best


def compute_direction(a, L, gamma, mu, noise_scale, rng):
    """
    Return a log-space direction v and current score.

    The direction is proportional to convolution of a weighted histogram of
    currently large coefficients with the current sequence.
    """
    a = as_1d_float(a)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros_like(a), float("inf")
    a = a / s
    n = a.size

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)[:2 * n - 1].real
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    np.clip(c, 0.0, None, out=c)
    m = c.max()
    if m <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(a), 0.0

    r = c / m
    if noise_scale > 0.0 and rng is not None:
        rn = r + float(noise_scale) * rng.standard_normal(len(r))
        rn = np.where(np.isfinite(rn), rn, 0.0)
        np.clip(rn, 0.0, None, out=rn)
        mn = rn.max()
        if mn > 0.0:
            r = rn / mn

    expv = np.exp(float(gamma) * (r - 1.0))
    ws = expv.sum()
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        expv = np.ones_like(r, dtype=np.float64)
        ws = expv.size
    w = expv / ws

    if mu > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - float(mu)) * w + float(mu) / len(w)

    W = np.fft.rfft(w, n=L)
    G = np.fft.irfft(W * A, n=L).real[:n]
    G = np.where(np.isfinite(G), G, 0.0)
    np.clip(G, 0.0, None, out=G)

    mean_a = a.mean()
    v = G / (mean_a + _EPS)
    v -= v.mean()

    # If the position is nearly stationary, inject a smooth antisymmetric escape mode.
    if np.std(v) < 1e-10:
        s0 = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * np.arange(n) / max(1, n))
        s0 = s0 - s0.mean()
        sd = s0.std()
        if sd > 0.0:
            v = 0.25 * s0 / sd

    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, -6.0, 6.0)
    return v, float(2.0 * n * m)


def candidate_from_x(x, L, n):
    """Convert log-coefficients to a normalized sequence and score it."""
    if x.size != n:
        x = np.resize(x, n)
    z = x - x.max()
    z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None, float("inf")
    a = e / s
    sc = score_sequence_fft_L(a, L)
    return a, float(sc)


def remaining_until(deadline):
    """Seconds remaining until a monotonic deadline."""
    return max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())


def full_grid_optimize(
    start,
    budget_s,
    seed=0,
    gamma_start=6.0,
    gamma_end=90.0,
    eta_start=0.8,
    mu_start=0.18,
):
    """
    Log-space multiplicative local search.

    x = log(a), and a step is x <- x - eta*v followed by softmax renormalization.
    """
    start = normalize_nonnegative(start)
    n = start.size
    L = next_pow2_int(2 * n - 1)

    cur = start.copy()
    cur_score = score_sequence_fft_L(cur, L)
    best = cur.copy()
    best_score = cur_score

    if budget_s <= 0.0 or n < 2:
        return best, best_score

    x = np.log(cur + _TINY)
    x = x - x.max()
    x = np.clip(x, -30.0, 30.0)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    eta = float(eta_start)
    no_improve = 0

    stage_fracs = [0.45, 0.35, 0.20]
    for idx, frac in enumerate(stage_fracs):
        if remaining_until(deadline) <= 5.0:
            break
        stage_end = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + remaining_until(deadline) * float(frac))
        gamma = float(gamma_start) * (float(gamma_end) / float(gamma_start)) ** (
            idx / max(1, len(stage_fracs) - 1)
        )
        mu = max(0.02, float(mu_start) * (1.0 - 0.5 * idx / len(stage_fracs)))

        while time.monotonic() < stage_end:
            v, cur_score = compute_direction(cur, L, gamma, mu, 0.0, rng)
            if not np.isfinite(cur_score) or not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
                no_improve += 100
                continue

            accepted = False
            for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
                x_trial = x - (eta * float(fac)) * v
                b, sc = candidate_from_x(x_trial, L, n)
                if b is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
                    cur = b
                    x = np.log(cur + _TINY)
                    x = x - x.max()
                    x = np.clip(x, -30.0, 30.0)
                    cur_score = sc
                    accepted = True
                    break

            if accepted:
                no_improve = 0
                if cur_score < best_score - 1e-10:
                    best = cur.copy()
                    best_score = cur_score
                    if best_score < 1.999999:
                        print(f"  full-grid internal best n={n} score={best_score:.12f}")
                eta = min(2.5, eta * 1.03)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                eta = max(0.04, eta * 0.95)
                if no_improve > 700:
                    xb = np.log(best + _TINY)
                    xb = xb - xb.max()
                    xb = np.clip(xb, -30.0, 30.0)
                    pert = rng.normal(0.0, 0.18, size=n)
                    x = xb + pert
                    cur = softmax_from_x(x)
                    cur_score = score_sequence_fft_L(cur, L)
                    no_improve = 0
                    eta = float(eta_start)

    return best, best_score


def local_perturb_sequence(start, budget_s, seed=0):
    """Cheap block-multiplicative local search."""
    a = normalize_nonnegative(start)
    n = a.size
    if n < 2 or budget_s <= 0.0:
        return a, score_sequence_fft(a)

    L = next_pow2_int(2 * n - 1)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence_fft_L(best, L)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    min_w = max(2, n // 5000)
    max_w = max(min_w + 1, n // 20 + 1)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        width = int(rng.integers(min_w, max_w))
        width = min(width, n)
        start_i = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
        cand = best.copy()
        cand[start_i:start_i + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.07, size=width))
        if rng.random() < 0.30:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
        cand = normalize_nonnegative(cand)
        sc = score_sequence_fft_L(cand, L)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best = cand
            best_sc = sc
            if best_sc < 1.999999:
                print(f"  local perturb internal best n={n} score={best_sc:.12f}")
    return best, best_sc


def build_convolution_a_matrix(a):
    """Dense matrix A for the constraints A*g <= M, where (f*g)_k = sum_i A[k,i] g_i."""
    a = as_1d_float(a)
    n = a.size
    A = np.empty((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        i1 = min(k, n - 1) + 1
        A[k, i0:i1] = a[i0:i1]
    return A


def lp_refine_sequence(start, budget_s, seed=0, max_iters=250):
    """
    LP direction refinement:
      maximize sum(g) subject to g>=0 and conv(start,g) <= current_max.
    Then line-search a convex update start <- (1-t)start + t*g.
    """
    a = normalize_nonnegative(start)
    n = a.size
    if not HAVE_SCIPY_LP or n < 24 or n > 1100 or budget_s <= 0.0:
        return a, score_sequence_fft(a)

    L = next_pow2_int(2 * n - 1)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_sequence_fft_L(best, L)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    no_improve = 0

    obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    t_choices = [0.002, 0.005, 0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.7, 1.0]

    for _it in range(max(1, int(max_iters))):
        if remaining_until(deadline) < 5.0:
            break
        if no_improve > 18:
            break

        M = best_sc / (2.0 * n)
        if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        A = build_convolution_a_matrix(best)
        b = np.full(2 * n - 1, M * 1.00000001 + 1e-12, dtype=np.float64)

        try:
            res = _sp_linprog(
                obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={
                    "presolve": True,
                    "dual_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
                    "primal_feasibility_tolerance": 1e-9,
                    "dual_objective_tolerance": 1e-9,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res.x is None or res.status not in (0, 1):
            break

        g = as_1d_float(res.x)
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        gs = g.sum()
        if gs <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(gs):
            break
        g = g / gs

        cand_best = best
        cand_best_sc = best_sc
        for t in t_choices:
            mix = (1.0 - float(t)) * best + float(t) * g
            mix = normalize_nonnegative(mix)
            sc = score_sequence_fft_L(mix, L)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cand_best_sc - 1e-11:
                cand_best = mix
                cand_best_sc = sc

        if cand_best_sc < best_sc - 1e-9:
            best = cand_best
            best_sc = cand_best_sc
            no_improve = 0
            print(f"  LP internal best n={n} score={best_sc:.12f} iter={_it+1}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1

    return best, best_sc


def update_state(seq, score, state):
    """Update a [sequence, score] state object if seq is better."""
    seq = normalize_nonnegative(seq)
    if seq is None or seq.size == 0:
        return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        score = score_sequence_fft(seq)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    if score < state[1] - 1e-12:
        state[1] = float(score)
        state[0] = seq.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
        print(f"GLOBAL BEST n={seq.size} score={score:.12f}")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main search driver. Returns the best list found before the deadline."""
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2400.0
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        budget_s = 1.0

    reserve = min(10.0, 0.05 * max(1.0, budget_s))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, budget_s - reserve)
    print(f"Starting search with budget={budget_s:.1f}s")

    state = [None, float("inf")]

    # Use the previously found construction if it exists.
    hs_seq = None
    hs = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if hs is not None:
        hs_arr = as_1d_float(hs)
        hs_arr = np.maximum(hs_arr, 0.0)
        if hs_arr.size > 0 and hs_arr.sum() > 0.0:
            hs_norm = normalize_nonnegative(hs_arr)
            if hs_norm.size > 8000:
                hs_norm = upsample_sequence(hs_norm, 2048)
            hs_seq = hs_norm
            update_state(hs_seq, score_sequence_fft(hs_seq), state)

    if state[0] is None:
        base = make_start_sequence(1024, seed)
        update_state(base, score_sequence_fft(base), state)

    # 1. Low-dimensional Fourier initialization / polishing.
    if remaining_until(deadline) > 120.0:
        n = 512
        start = upsample_sequence(state[0], n) if state[0] is not None else make_start_sequence(n, seed)
        a, sc = optimize_fourier(n, 8, 14.0, seed + 1, start_seq=start, maxiter=120)
        update_state(a, sc, state)

        a, sc = optimize_fourier(n, 8, 45.0, seed + 2, start_seq=None, maxiter=90)
        update_state(a, sc, state)

    if remaining_until(deadline) > 120.0:
        n = 1024
        start = upsample_sequence(state[0], n) if state[0] is not None else make_start_sequence(n, seed)
        a, sc = optimize_fourier(n, 10, 20.0, seed + 3, start_seq=start, maxiter=70)
        update_state(a, sc, state)

    # 2. LP at moderate resolution.
    if remaining_until(deadline) > 220.0:
        n = 512
        start = upsample_sequence(state[0], n)
        a, sc = lp_refine_sequence(start, min(200.0, remaining_until(deadline) - 20.0), seed + 10)
        update_state(a, sc, state)

    # 3. Full-grid optimization at n=1024.
    if remaining_until(deadline) > 280.0:
        n = 1024
        start_up = upsample_sequence(state[0], n)
        start_fresh = make_start_sequence(n, seed + 20)
        if score_sequence_fft(start_fresh) < score_sequence_fft(start_up):
            start_up = start_fresh
        a, sc = full_grid_optimize(
            start_up,
            min(500.0, remaining_until(deadline) - 20.0),
            seed + 30,
            gamma_start=6.0,
            gamma_end=90.0,
            eta_start=0.85,
        )
        update_state(a, sc, state)

    # 4. LP at n=1024, if it seems worth it.
    if remaining_until(deadline) > 280.0:
        n = 1024
        start = upsample_sequence(state[0], n)
        a, sc = lp_refine_sequence(start, min(260.0, remaining_until(deadline) - 20.0), seed + 40, max_iters=180)
        update_state(a, sc, state)

    # 5. If the provided construction is a usable length, refine it directly.
    if hs_seq is not None and 1024 <= hs_seq.size <= 8000 and remaining_until(deadline) > 280.0:
        a, sc = full_grid_optimize(
            hs_seq,
            min(320.0, remaining_until(deadline) - 20.0),
            seed + 50,
            gamma_start=5.0,
            gamma_end=80.0,
            eta_start=0.8,
        )
        update_state(a, sc, state)

    # 6. Upsample to n=2048 and refine.
    if remaining_until(deadline) > 420.0:
        n = 2048
        start_up = upsample_sequence(state[0], n)
        start_fresh = make_start_sequence(n, seed + 60) if remaining_until(deadline) > 700.0 else None
        if start_fresh is not None and score_sequence_fft(start_fresh) < score_sequence_fft(start_up):
            start_up = start_fresh
        a, sc = full_grid_optimize(
            start_up,
            min(700.0, remaining_until(deadline) - 20.0),
            seed + 70,
            gamma_start=5.0,
            gamma_end=100.0,
            eta_start=0.85,
        )
        update_state(a, sc, state)

    # 7. Upsample to n=4096 and refine if there is still a comfortable budget.
    if remaining_until(deadline) > 320.0:
        n = 4096
        start_up = upsample_sequence(state[0], n)
        a, sc = full_grid_optimize(
            start_up,
            min(480.0, remaining_until(deadline) - 20.0),
            seed + 80,
            gamma_start=5.0,
            gamma_end=100.0,
            eta_start=0.8,
        )
        update_state(a, sc, state)

    # 8. Final cheap local perturbation pass.
    if remaining_until(deadline) > 70.0:
        a, sc = local_perturb_sequence(state[0], min(140.0, remaining_until(deadline) - 10.0), seed + 90)
        update_state(a, sc, state)

    seq = state[0]
    if seq is None:
        seq = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)

    seq = as_1d_float(seq)
    seq = np.maximum(seq, 0.0)
    s = seq.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        seq = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        s = seq.sum()
    seq = seq / s

    # Optional exact check. The external evaluator is authoritative.
    if remaining_until(deadline) > 25.0 and seq.size <= 6000:
        exact = safe_exact_score(seq)
        if np.isfinite(exact):
            print(f"Final exact score from evaluate_sequence: {exact:.12f}")

    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = seq.sum()
    if s < 0.01:
        if s > 0.0:
            seq = seq * (0.02 / s)
        else:
            seq = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(v) for v in seq]
```
